\\nAND \u2014 retrieves results containing all specified terms.

\\nOR \u2014 retrieves results containing at least one of the specified terms.

\\nAND NOT \u2014 retrieves results containing the first term, excluding those that also contain the term specified after the operator.

\\nCurly braces { } are used to group terms and establish priority among search conditions.

\\n1. Start the search with the = sign
\\nExample: =Education OR learning
\\nThe search will retrieve records containing Education or Learning.

\\n2. Start the search directly with {
\\nIn this case, the corresponding closing } is required.
\\nExample: {Education OR learning}

\\nAND \u2014 combine subjects
\\nExample: =research AND outreach
\\nRetrieves records containing the terms research and outreach.

\\nAND NOT \u2014 exclude terms
\\nThe AND NOT operator restricts results by removing records containing a specific term.
\\nExample: =research AND NOT outreach
\\nRetrieves records containing the term research and excludes results that also contain the term outreach.
